# جميع مطالبات الصور المتبقية

## pattern-9039 → pattern-9039-v1.png

Photorealistic everyday editorial photography; warm natural light matching STYLE-REFERENCE.png. Atlas: portrait EXACT3:4,3columns by4rows of equal SQUARE cells, row-major LEFT-to-RIGHT then top-to-bottom. Narrow continuous straight white gutters. Final display1536x2048, each cell512x512. Source pixels may differ only if its aspect and square-cell geometry remain correct. Keep essential people/props40px inside each512px cell. Never stretch a1:2 or4x4 output. Single-cell alternative: one square image, no grid. No words, captions, numbers, logos, bubbles, inset pictures, arrows or drawn sound rays. Analog clock hands/tick marks only. STYLE-REFERENCE is layout/style only: replace every scene with the requested content.

Conservative Arab Muslim family learning contexts. Men wear loose opaque long-sleeved high-neck shirts and full-length trousers or thobes. Women wear loose opaque full-coverage clothing and hijab. Apply modest clothing to background people too.
No bare torso, shoulders, midriff or thighs, tight or transparent clothes, revealing nightwear, swimwear, underwear, changing, bathing, showering, intimate or romantic poses, kissing or inappropriate physical contact.
Water and beach contexts use fully clothed people on a dry shore, promenade or empty poolside. Never show swimmers, sunbathers, mixed bathing or distant revealing figures. Depict swimming discussions before the activity, with equipment where useful.
No alcohol, gambling, nightclub setting, graphic injury, active weapon violence, or religious caricatures. Use ordinary respectful Arab settings without adding religious rituals unrelated to the sentence.
Preserve the exact English sentence and Arabic meaning. Adapt only the visual staging. If a compliant scene cannot convey the meaning honestly, hold the cell for review instead of substituting an unrelated picture.
No captions, words, numbers, logos, speech/thought bubbles, graphic overlays or floating inset pictures. Physical analog clocks can use hands and tick marks for time examples.
Exactly three columns and four rows in row-major order, twelve square cells, portrait 3:4, final atlas 1536x2048, 512x512 per cell with 40px safe inset and continuous straight white dividers. Measure actual source gutters before preparing the display atlas.
Additional coverage: all sleeves extend loosely to the wrists, full-length opaque garments; women opaque hijab. Inspect tiny background people too. Same-gender adults for ordinary practical help where appropriate. Children, if needed, fully modestly covered. No actual bathing, changing, swimmers or revealing distant figures. Depict delicate meanings before/after the activity from dry land or outside a closed private door. Preserve the sentence; hold unclear scenes instead of changing its meaning.

Output filename: pattern-9039-v1.png
Use the image-generation tool, not programmatic drawings or fetched stock photos. English/Arabic below are context ONLY; never render their text. Exact panel order:
1. pattern-9039-01
English: Is it okay if I open the window?
Arabic meaning: هل من المقبول إذا فتحت النافذة؟
Visual staging: An adult politely asking another adult for permission. Specific context: Is it okay if I open the window? No captions or written speech.

2. pattern-9039-02
English: Is it okay if we sit here?
Arabic meaning: هل من المقبول إذا جلسنا هنا؟
Visual staging: An adult politely asking another adult for permission. Specific context: Is it okay if we sit here? No captions or written speech.

3. pattern-9039-03
English: Is it okay if I call you later?
Arabic meaning: هل يناسبك إذا اتصلت بك لاحقًا؟
Visual staging: An adult politely asking another adult for permission. Specific context: Is it okay if I call you later? No captions or written speech.

4. pattern-9039-04
English: Is it okay if I take a photo?
Arabic meaning: هل من المقبول إذا التقطت صورة؟
Visual staging: An adult politely asking another adult for permission. Specific context: Is it okay if I take a photo? No captions or written speech.

5. pattern-9039-05
English: Is it okay if I use this chair?
Arabic meaning: هل تسمح لي باستخدام هذا الكرسي؟
Visual staging: An adult asking a seated person about an empty chair.

6. pattern-9039-06
English: Is it okay if we start five minutes later?
Arabic meaning: هل يناسبك أن نبدأ بعد خمس دقائق؟
Visual staging: Two colleagues checking their watches before a meeting.

7. pattern-9039-07
English: Is it okay if I bring my younger brother?
Arabic meaning: هل من المقبول أن أحضر أخي الأصغر؟
Visual staging: An adult asking a host about bringing a younger brother.

8. pattern-9039-08
English: Is it okay if she waits in the reception area?
Arabic meaning: هل يُسمح لها بالانتظار في منطقة الاستقبال؟
Visual staging: An adult asking a receptionist about a waiting visitor.

9. pattern-9039-09
English: Is it okay if I charge my phone here?
Arabic meaning: هل تسمح لي بشحن هاتفي هنا؟
Visual staging: An adult holding a phone charger beside an unused socket.

10. pattern-9039-10
English: Is it okay if we share this table?
Arabic meaning: هل يمكننا مشاركة هذه الطاولة؟
Visual staging: Two cafe visitors asking to share a table with another customer.

11. pattern-9039-11
English: Is it okay if I leave my bag here until lunch?
Arabic meaning: هل يُسمح لي بترك حقيبتي هنا حتى الغداء؟
Visual staging: An adult asking a staff member where to leave a bag.

12. pattern-9039-12
English: Is it okay if I ask one more question before you go?
Arabic meaning: هل تسمح لي بطرح سؤال آخر قبل أن تذهب؟
Visual staging: An adult politely stopping a departing colleague to ask a question.

Before returning, inspect all foreground/background people, props and panel order. Report any unresolved mismatch and provide the actual generated file; never say a missing image is done. If the grid repeatedly fails, return twelve genuinely generated square panels using the listed alternative filenames and explain the switch.

1. pattern-9040-01
English: You may enter the room now.
Arabic meaning: يمكنك دخول الغرفة الآن.
Visual staging: A staff member allowing a visitor into a room.

2. pattern-9040-02
English: You may use this computer.
Arabic meaning: يُسمح لك باستخدام هذا الحاسوب.
Visual staging: A staff member giving a visitor permission to use a computer.

3. pattern-9040-03
English: You may leave after the lesson.
Arabic meaning: يمكنك المغادرة بعد الدرس.
Visual staging: A teacher giving adult learners permission to leave after class.

4. pattern-9040-04
English: You may borrow this book.
Arabic meaning: يمكنك استعارة هذا الكتاب.
Visual staging: A librarian permitting a visitor to borrow a book.

5. pattern-9040-05
English: You may take a short break now.
Arabic meaning: يُسمح لك بأخذ استراحة قصيرة الآن.
Visual staging: A supervisor giving a worker permission to take a break.

6. pattern-9040-06
English: You may sit anywhere in this waiting area.
Arabic meaning: يُسمح لك بالجلوس في أي مكان في منطقة الانتظار هذه.
Visual staging: A receptionist showing a visitor the available waiting seats.

7. pattern-9040-07
English: You may keep the spare copy.
Arabic meaning: يُسمح لك بالاحتفاظ بالنسخة الإضافية.
Visual staging: A teacher handing an adult learner an extra copy of a worksheet.

8. pattern-9040-08
English: You may use a dictionary during this activity.
Arabic meaning: يُسمح لك باستخدام قاموس خلال هذا النشاط.
Visual staging: A teacher indicating an open dictionary during an exercise.

9. pattern-9040-09
English: You may bring one guest to the event.
Arabic meaning: يُسمح لك بإحضار ضيف واحد إلى الفعالية.
Visual staging: An event organizer explaining an invitation to an adult guest.

10. pattern-9040-10
English: You may park here after six.
Arabic meaning: يُسمح لك بإيقاف سيارتك هنا بعد السادسة.
Visual staging: A parking attendant showing a driver an available parking space.

11. pattern-9040-11
English: You may ask for more time if you need it.
Arabic meaning: يمكنك طلب وقت إضافي إذا احتجت إليه.
Visual staging: A teacher offering an adult learner additional time.

12. pattern-9040-12
English: You may leave when you finish, but please return the key first.
Arabic meaning: يُسمح لك بالمغادرة عندما تنتهي، لكن أعد المفتاح أولًا من فضلك.
Visual staging: A supervisor indicating a return-key tray near an exit.

English: I don't have to work today.
Arabic meaning: لست مضطرًا للعمل اليوم.

English: I don't have to bring my laptop.

English: I don't have to pay for this ticket.
Arabic meaning: لست مضطرًا لدفع ثمن هذه التذكرة.
Visual staging: An adult explaining that an activity is optional. Specific context: I don't have to pay for this ticket. No captions or written speech.

Arabic meaning: لست مضطرًا للقيادة لأن المحطة قريبة.

6. pattern-9044-06

Arabic meaning: لست مضطرًا لارتداء زي موحد في هذه الوظيفة.
Visual staging: An adult choosing ordinary work clothes from a wardrobe.

7. pattern-9044-07

Arabic meaning: لست مضطرًا للطهي الليلة؛ فأختي تعد العشاء.
Visual staging: An adult relaxing while a sister prepares dinner in a kitchen.

8. pattern-9044-08

Arabic meaning: لست مضطرًا للإجابة عن كل سؤال فورًا.
Visual staging: An adult pausing thoughtfully during a discussion.

9. pattern-9044-09
English: I don't have to print the form; I can send it online.
Arabic meaning: لست مضطرًا لطباعة الاستمارة؛ إذ يمكنني إرسالها إلكترونيًا.
Visual staging: An adult submitting a form on a laptop beside an unused printer.

Arabic meaning: لست مضطرًا لشراء نسخة جديدة لأن المكتبة لديها نسخة.
Visual staging: An adult borrowing a book at a library counter.

11. pattern-9044-11
English: I don't have to finish this until Friday.
Arabic meaning: لست مضطرًا لإنهاء هذا قبل يوم الجمعة.
Visual staging: An adult checking a later deadline on a work calendar.

12. pattern-9044-12
English: I don't have to attend, but I can go if I am interested.
Arabic meaning: لست مضطرًا للحضور، لكن يمكنني الذهاب إذا كنت مهتمًا.
Visual staging: An adult considering an optional workshop invitation.

No bare torso, shoulders, midriff or thighs, tight or transparent clothes, revealing nightwear, swimwear, underwear, changing, bathing, showering, intimate or romantic poses, kissing or inappropriate physical contact.
Water and beach contexts use fully clothed people on a dry shore, promenade or empty poolside. Never show swimmers, sunbathers, mixed bathing or distant revealing figures. Depict swimming discussions before the activity, with equipment where useful.
No alcohol, gambling, nightclub setting, graphic injury, active weapon violence, or religious caricatures. Use ordinary respectful Arab settings without adding religious rituals unrelated to the sentence.
Preserve the exact English sentence and Arabic meaning. Adapt only the visual staging. If a compliant scene cannot convey the meaning honestly, hold the cell for review instead of substituting an unrelated picture.
No captions, words, numbers, logos, speech/thought bubbles, graphic overlays or floating inset pictures. Physical analog clocks can use hands and tick marks for time examples.
Exactly three columns and four rows in row-major order, twelve square cells, portrait 3:4, final atlas 1536x2048, 512x512 per cell with 40px safe inset and continuous straight white dividers. Measure actual source gutters before preparing the display atlas.
Additional coverage: all sleeves extend loosely to the wrists, full-length opaque garments; women opaque hijab. Inspect tiny background people too. Same-gender adults for ordinary practical help where appropriate. Children, if needed, fully modestly covered. No actual bathing, changing, swimmers or revealing distant figures. Depict delicate meanings before/after the activity from dry land or outside a closed private door. Preserve the sentence; hold unclear scenes instead of changing its meaning.

Atlas: portrait EXACT3:4,3columns by4rows of equal SQUARE cells, row-major LEFT-to-RIGHT then top-to-bottom. Narrow continuous straight white gutters. Final display1536x2048, each cell512x512. Single-cell alternative: one square image, no grid. No words, captions, numbers, logos, bubbles, inset pictures, arrows or drawn sound rays. Analog clock hands/tick marks only. STYLE-REFERENCE is layout/style only: replace every scene with the requested content.

Conservative Arab Muslim family learning contexts. Men wear loose opaque long-sleeved high-neck shirts and full-length trousers or thobes. Women wear loose opaque full-coverage clothing and hijab. Apply modest clothing to background people too.
No bare torso, shoulders, midriff or thighs, tight or transparent clothes, revealing nightwear, swimwear, underwear, changing, bathing, showering, intimate or romantic poses, kissing or inappropriate physical contact.
Water and beach contexts use fully clothed people on a dry shore, promenade or empty poolside. Never show swimmers, sunbathers, mixed bathing or distant revealing figures. Depict swimming discussions before the activity, with equipment where useful.
No alcohol, gambling, nightclub setting, graphic injury, active weapon violence, or religious caricatures. Use ordinary respectful Arab settings without adding religious rituals unrelated to the sentence.
Preserve the exact English sentence and Arabic meaning. Adapt only the visual staging. If a compliant scene cannot convey the meaning honestly, hold the cell for review instead of substituting an unrelated picture.
No captions, words, numbers, logos, speech/thought bubbles, graphic overlays or floating inset pictures. Physical analog clocks can use hands and tick marks for time examples.
Exactly three columns and four rows in row-major order, twelve square cells, portrait 3:4, final atlas 1536x2048, 512x512 per cell with 40px safe inset and continuous straight white dividers. Measure actual source gutters before preparing the display atlas.
Additional coverage: all sleeves extend loosely to the wrists, full-length opaque garments; women opaque hijab. Inspect tiny background people too. Same-gender adults for ordinary practical help where appropriate. Children, if needed, fully modestly covered. No actual bathing, changing, swimmers or revealing distant figures. Depict delicate meanings before/after the activity from dry land or outside a closed private door. Preserve the sentence; hold unclear scenes instead of changing its meaning.

Arabic meaning: كنا جائعين، لذا أعددنا الغداء.
Visual staging: Friends preparing lunch when hungry

4. pattern-9142-04
English: The shop was closed, so we went home.
Arabic meaning: كان المتجر مغلقًا، لذلك عدنا إلى المنزل.
Visual staging: Shoppers outside a closed shop

5. pattern-9142-05
English: The bus was full, so we waited for the next one.
Arabic meaning: كانت الحافلة ممتلئة، لذا انتظرنا التالية.
Visual staging: Travelers waiting as a crowded bus departs

6. pattern-9142-06
English: I couldn't reach the shelf, so I asked for help.
Arabic meaning: لم أستطع الوصول إلى الرف، لذا طلبت المساعدة.
Visual staging: Adult seeking help beside a high shelf

7. pattern-9142-07
English: She lost her key, so she called the landlord.
Arabic meaning: فقدت مفتاحها، لذا اتصلت بمالك المنزل.
Visual staging: Woman calling beside a locked apartment

8. pattern-9142-08
English: We had extra vegetables, so we made soup.
Arabic meaning: كانت لدينا خضروات زائدة، لذا أعددنا حساءً.
Visual staging: Family cooking soup with vegetables

9. pattern-9142-09
English: The room was too dark, so I opened the curtains.
Arabic meaning: كانت الغرفة مظلمة جدًا، لذا فتحت الستائر.
Visual staging: Adult opening curtains in a dark room

10. pattern-9142-10
English: He wanted to improve his pronunciation, so he recorded himself.
Arabic meaning: أراد تحسين نطقه، لذا سجل صوته.
Visual staging: Learner recording speech with a phone

11. pattern-9142-11
English: The path was slippery, so we walked slowly.
Arabic meaning: كان الممر زلقًا، لذا مشينا ببطء.
Visual staging: Walkers carefully crossing a wet path

12. pattern-9142-12
English: Our team finished early, so we helped the others.
Arabic meaning: أنهى فريقنا العمل مبكرًا، لذا ساعدنا الآخرين.
Visual staging: Volunteers joining another team's task

Conservative Arab Muslim family learning contexts. Men wear loose opaque long-sleeved high-neck shirts and full-length trousers or thobes. Women wear loose opaque full-coverage clothing and hijab. Apply modest clothing to background people too.
No bare torso, shoulders, midriff or thighs, tight or transparent clothes, revealing nightwear, swimwear, underwear, changing, bathing, showering, intimate or romantic poses, kissing or inappropriate physical contact.

English: I missed the bus. That's why I'm late.
Arabic meaning: فاتتني الحافلة. لهذا السبب تأخرت.
Visual staging: Late learner arriving after missing a bus

2. pattern-9143-02
English: A fallen tree blocked the road. That's why we turned back.
Arabic meaning: سدت شجرة ساقطة الطريق. لهذا السبب عدنا.
Visual staging: Travelers turning back before a fallen tree

3. pattern-9143-03
English: She trained daily. That's why she improved.
Arabic meaning: تدربت يوميًا. ولهذا تحسنت.
Visual staging: Athlete improving with practice

4. pattern-9143-04
English: The room was noisy. That's why I moved.
Arabic meaning: كانت الغرفة صاخبة. ولهذا انتقلت.
Visual staging: Adult leaving a noisy room

5. pattern-9143-05
English: My phone battery died. That's why I didn't reply.
Arabic meaning: نفدت بطارية هاتفي. لهذا السبب لم أرد.

6. pattern-9143-06
English: The library is quiet. That's why I study there.
Arabic meaning: المكتبة هادئة. لهذا السبب أدرس هناك.
Visual staging: Learner reading peacefully in a library

7. pattern-9143-07
English: She knows the route well. That's why she's leading the walk.
Arabic meaning: تعرف الطريق جيدًا. لهذا السبب تقود المجموعة في المشي.
Visual staging: Woman guiding walkers along a trail

8. pattern-9143-08
English: I washed the floor. That's why it's still wet.
Arabic meaning: غسلت الأرضية. لهذا السبب ما زالت مبتلة.
Visual staging: Adult holding a mop beside a wet floor

9. pattern-9143-09
English: The train leaves soon. That's why we're hurrying.
Arabic meaning: يغادر القطار قريبًا. لهذا السبب نحن مستعجلون.
Visual staging: Travelers hurrying toward a platform

10. pattern-9143-10
English: He forgot his glasses. That's why he can't read the menu.
Arabic meaning: نسي نظارته. لهذا السبب لا يستطيع قراءة قائمة الطعام.
Visual staging: Diner struggling to read a menu

11. pattern-9143-11
English: We saved a little every week. That's why we can afford the trip.
Arabic meaning: ادخرنا قليلًا كل أسبوع. لهذا السبب نستطيع تحمل تكلفة الرحلة.
Visual staging: Couple reviewing savings and travel plans Use respectful conversational distance and neutral gestures, no romantic staging or physical intimacy.

12. pattern-9143-12
English: I misunderstood the date. That's why I came a day early.
Arabic meaning: فهمت التاريخ خطأ. لهذا السبب جئت قبل الموعد بيوم.
Visual staging: Visitor checking an appointment at reception

English: Even though I was afraid, I asked the question.

5. pattern-9147-05
English: Even though the beach was crowded, we found a quiet corner.
Arabic meaning: مع أن الشاطئ كان مزدحمًا، وجدنا ركنًا هادئًا.
Visual staging: A modestly dressed Arab family finding a quiet shaded corner of a coastal promenade, with a busier fully clothed crowd far along the promenade and sea beyond. No swimmers, sunbathers, romance or swimwear. Stage the entire scene fully clothed on dry land; empty water, no swimmers, sunbathers or revealing background figures. Use respectful conversational distance and neutral gestures, no romantic staging or physical intimacy.

6. pattern-9147-06
English: Even though I knew the answer, I waited for my turn.
Arabic meaning: مع أنني كنت أعرف الإجابة، انتظرت دوري.

Arabic meaning: مع أن وقتها كان قليلًا، أعدت وجبة شهية.
Visual staging: Woman serving a freshly prepared meal

Arabic meaning: مع أننا اختلفنا، عامل بعضنا بعضًا باحترام.
